## [Developer] 2026-08-23 — FIX-CHEF-MARKER-KEY-ANCHOR-1 (cross-service/, developer, P0 S; parent FIX-CHEF-MARKER-KEY-WINDOW-ANCHOR, design `docs/architecture-briefs/2026-08-06-cowork-marker-lifecycle-anchor-and-release.md` §2 Component A bullet 1): live `MATCHES` entries from `scripts/agents-flow/cowork-match-slots.js` now carry `scheduled_utc_time` — the cron's own nominal fire instant (ISO8601 Zulu, timezone-free). Root cause it unblocks: every guaranteed-slot flow derives its published-marker dedup key from a `date` call the EXECUTING AGENT makes at whatever instant it runs, so two peers in the same window derive different keys and a late retry re-targets a different day. Confirmed live twice — 2026-07-22 chef-evening double-published on `:2026-07-22` and `:2026-07-23` from ONE instant straddling VN midnight; 2026-08-05 chef-evening was retried after a 10h42m macOS host sleep, keyed `:2026-08-06`, permanently missed the 08-05 dish and published a degraded "evening" dish at 13:37 VN. `catchup_raw` has carried the correct anchor since TASK-COWORK-CATCHUP-2 — live matches did not, so the two paths could never agree on a key. New exported `annotateScheduledUtc(slots, nowUnix, deps)` REUSES the predicate's own `mostRecentCronFireBefore` with the matcher's own `field`/`dowMatch` (one derivation, not two — re-implementing the reverse-cron walk would recreate the very bug class this closes). Applied at the CLI (like `catchup_raw`) because `matchSlots()`'s test seam is a `{actualM,H,DOM,MON,DOW}` ctx with no absolute instant, and every real consumer reads CLI stdout. Degrades to `null` (never throws) on a missing predicate module, absent/malformed cron, or no fire within the 8-day bounded lookback. Test: `node scripts/agents-flow/cowork-match-slots.test.js` 90/90 GREEN (69 baseline + 21 new TC-28..TC-34, including the two real incident windows and the key-agreement invariant). Siblings unregressed: catchup-predicate 34/34, guaranteed-slot-firer 53/53, tick-preflight 75/75. Live CLI on the real schedule emitted `digest-sunday cron="47 13 * * 0" scheduled_utc_time="2026-08-23T13:47:00.000Z"`. ANCHOR-2/3/4 (the consumers: `match-slots.md` schema doc, `spawn-fanout.md` prompt token, `chef.md` parse) are all inside `docs/agents/` = agent-father's `commit_zone` — re-routed `next_agent` developer -> agent-father rather than breached.

## [Developer] 2026-08-23 — TASK-CRON-LIVENESS-PROBE-SCRIPT + TASK-CRON-LIVENESS-PROBE-TESTS (scripts/agents-flow/, developer, P0 M+M; parent FIX-CRON-REARM-STEP1B1-LIVENESS-ORACLE-BLIND-WINDOW-FALSE-LIVE, brief `docs/architecture-briefs/2026-08-23-cron-rearm-liveness-oracle-process-observation.md`): new `scripts/agents-flow/cron-marker-liveness-probe.sh` + `.test.sh`. Root cause: the three `/cron-*` re-arm skills share ONE Step 1b.1 guard shape with THREE different arbitrary `orphan_threshold_seconds`, and BOTH of its oracles read bookkeeping the dying session wrote about itself — `session-presence` is a participation signal (measured 2 roster rows against 5 live `claude` PIDs) and `task_force_release_orphan` reads `heartbeat_at` on the same marker row, so it answers "was alive T ago", never "is alive". One defect, two faces: false-LIVE at T=7200 (the 8h10m cowork-dispatcher outage of 2026-08-23, where the corpse's heartbeat was 328s old — maximally fresh — because the operator ran `/cron-cowork-team` 15s after relaunching the CLI) and false-DEAD at T=120 (standalone double-arm, confirmed 2x in memory). No threshold value fixes this. The probe OBSERVES THE OS instead: O1 recorded PID absent from `ps` (or present with a different `(pid,start_epoch,comm)` triple) soundly proves DEAD with no blind window; O2 transcript mtime inside a window proves LIVE only; O3 heartbeat/roster proves neither and is emitted as corroboration that can never decide. O1 outranks O2 — at the incident instant the dead session's transcript was 16s old, so any O2-primary design returns LIVE and reproduces the outage with a smaller constant. Mandatory third branch UNKNOWN (a two-branch LIVE/DEAD shape must guess, and it guesses LIVE), resolved by the measured `has_fire_election_mutex` property held in ONE table inside the probe — cowork/detect-loop `steal`, standalone `defer`+alarm. No branch terminates silently: UNKNOWN/DEFER emit a BUG telegram AND a `docs/signals/` row deduped on `(family, marker_owner_session)` with a 6h cooldown, reusing the existing open-signal convention. All six measured traps handled at their own sites with the trap named, each pinned by a static assertion in the gate (LC_ALL=C on BOTH `ps` and `date -j -f`; no macOS-invalid `etimes`; never test `ps` by exit code through a pipe; `stat -f '%m'` never `%Sm`; full triple vs PID reuse; read the transcript path from the marker, never re-derive the cwd encoding). Test: `bash scripts/agents-flow/cron-marker-liveness-probe.test.sh` 87/87 GREEN, fully mocked (zero real ps/stat/network), running under `LC_TIME=fr_FR.UTF-8` so every case doubles as a locale regression. F1 (2026-08-23 corpse, fresh heartbeat + FRESH 16s transcript + dead pid -> DEAD) and F2 (live standalone, 30948s-stale heartbeat + live pid -> LIVE) pass under ONE spec — that pair is the parent row's whole thesis. MUTATION-VERIFIED non-vacuous: hoisting the O2 check above O1 on a scratch copy makes the mutant answer LIVE for the corpse (exactly the outage) and F1 catches it, 3 assertions flip red; restored, 87/87. Live-verified against the real lock plane: family=cowork-team -> SELF (marker owned by this session, exit 0), detect-loop -> LIVE via O2 (marker has NO `registering_process` at all, confirming the brief's "O1 has zero wiring for 2 of 3 families"), standalone-team -> UNKNOWN + alarm (pre-v2 fingerprint, 5434s-stale transcript) — the live cowork marker still carries the broken v1 string `ppid-42648-start-Dim_23_aoû_02:40:51_2026_-host-admins-MBP.lan` exactly as the brief documented. NOT DONE HERE (agent-father's exclusive `commit_zone`, sequenced AFTER these two per the brief): the three `.claude/skills/cron-*/SKILL.md` Step 1b.1 + Step 1c payload edits (`TASK-CRON-SKILLMD-PROBE-WIRING`) — until they land the probe is shipped and tested but not yet invoked by any skill, and F5 (the SKILL.md wiring fixture) is deliberately that task's AC, not this one's.

## [Developer] 2026-08-23 — FIX-DEVTEAM-INCIDENT-LANE-CONSUMER-SCRIPTS (scripts/, developer, P0 M; sprint FIX-READYLANE-NO-SEVERITY-EXPEDITE-FIFO-BURIES-INCIDENT-P0, design `docs/architecture-briefs/2026-08-14-readylane-incident-lane-throughput.md` §4a-§4c): `ready[]` had exactly ONE generic consumer (RLC), claiming ONE row per invocation and only on its ~1-in-6 rotation turn — a throughput ceiling of roughly one row per six ticks against a 68-row queue, so an urgent row stays buried no matter how it is labelled. The architect measured that ORDERING-ONLY fixes (comparator changes, new expedite fields) cannot move a BINDING THROUGHPUT constraint. New `scripts/devteam-backlog-claim-incident-lane-consumer.jq` reuses QA-Drain's proven throughput shape (batch claim + dedicated budget; 226→56 on the structurally identical starvation problem). Selector is PO's already-live `po_expedited_at` (5 live rows, 0 code consumers before this) rather than a new field — extend, don't duplicate, and it makes an existing PO triage habit load-bearing instead of orphaned. Sort `[rank, po_expedited_at, idx]`: priority first (a stray P1-expedited row never jumps a P0-expedited one), then OLDEST-expedite-first so a freshly-marked incident cannot perpetually cut ahead inside the pool. **Severity changes throughput priority, never safety gating** — supervised / plan_only / epic-wrapper / deps_satisfied all stay unrelaxed. Two new predicates in `scripts/lib/devteam-eligibility.jq`: `is_po_expedited` (board-only, documented simplification — no detail-authored expedite has ever been observed) and `incident_wip_in_progress` (counts only this lane's DISTINCT `claimed_by` marker, so incident rows consume neither the shared `WIP<=2` slot nor compete for it; `INCIDENT_CAP=2` bounds them). `.head` uses the same `$head_free` conditional guard as every sibling — no new write pattern, so the single-linear head-writer collision-freedom proof is untouched. RLC gets a header cross-reference only, no logic change. Instrument: `scripts/audits/devteam-dispatch-gate-satisfiability.sh` gains a 13-assertion ILC section (buried-expedited POSITIVE, non-expedited-P0 NEGATIVE, ordering, safety-gates-unrelaxed, CAP counter + gate + queue-not-drop, WIP-independence under a saturated shared budget, head-busy negative control + positive half, and a true-no-op re-run check). **THREE PRE-EXISTING FALSE-REDS IN THAT INSTRUMENT FIXED IN PASSING (it was RED at baseline, 4 failures, none caused by this row — each root cause verified, not guessed):** (a) AC-EVICT-1/3 — the fixture wrote synthetic `DONE_VERIFIED` rows with no `verification.raw_probe`, which `checkVerificationGate` (added 2026-08-08, after the fixture was authored) hard-rejects, so `orch-cold-evict.sh` aborted at its own write gate and the eviction guard under test was never reached; reproduced identically against both the current and the pre-Component-6 cold-evict, proving the rot was in the fixture. (b) BOUNDED-1's branch tested the RAW `in_progress[]` length while the gate under test uses `wip_in_progress`, which EXCLUDES BLOCKED rows — the live board's first in_progress row was BLOCKED, so effective WIP was 0 and BOUNDED-1 correctly fired against an expectation that was itself wrong. (c) AC-DRS-HEAD-GUARD appended its synthetic row to the shared live-derived fixture, but the DRS claim takes `$swept[0]` and the live board carried 3 earlier `promoted_by: "dev-team (design-router sweep)"` rows, so DRS claimed a live row instead — live-board contamination, rebuilt as an isolated fixture like every sibling HEAD-GUARD block. Suite now 116 PASS / 0 FAIL, exit 0 (was exit 1). Siblings unregressed: `devteam-eligibility-resolved-secondary-dispatch-target.test.sh` 5/5, `test-devteam-donelane-drain.sh` 42/42, and the shared lib re-verified compiling against the live board immediately after the edit (the peer dispatcher reads it every tick). NOT DONE HERE: the `docs/agents/dev-team/flow/main.md` call site is agent-father's exclusive `commit_zone` and is the separate blocked row `FIX-DEVTEAM-INCIDENT-LANE-CONSUMER-MAINFLOW` — until it lands the ILC is shipped and tested but invoked on no tick.

## [Developer] 2026-08-23 — FIX-ORCHSTATE-MINT-FLAGGED-ROW-WITHOUT-RESOLVABLE-HANDLER (cross-service/, developer, P2; architect brief `docs/architecture-briefs/2026-08-23-fix-orchstate-mint-flagged-row-no-handler.md`, design phase (1) only): new NON-FATAL Stage 1i in `scripts/orch-validate.mjs` reporting rows that carry `supervised` and/or `plan_only` while `next_agent` AND `owner` are both empty in BOTH the board row and `backlog-detail.json` — a row asserting a deliberate-dispatch lane that no mechanism can route it through. 4 live rows aged 15-58 days before being hand-repaired on 2026-08-14, with nothing preventing the next one. The invariant is NOT "every row needs a handler": `owner`/`next_agent` absent with NEITHER flag set is a legitimate documented parked state and is never reported — the flag is what turns it into a contradiction. Trigger is OR, not AND: 3 of the 4 live violators carried only ONE flag. Predicate is MIRRORED, not reinvented — only the two-line detail-first/board-fallback lookups from `scripts/lib/devteam-eligibility.jq` (`effective_supervised` / `effective_plan_only` / `effective_owner` / `effective_next_agent`, as composed by `scripts/audits/bounded1-supervised-lane-report.sh`'s `dispatch_lane`) are ported into JS, with the jq file cited in-code as the algorithm SSOT and the known two-implementations coupling flagged. Placed physically BEFORE Stage 1h despite the later number, because 1h can `exit(2)` in reject mode and a non-fatal report downstream of that would be silently skipped on exactly the runs where board health matters most. Non-fatal by design with a dated promotion path (brief §4) — `orchStateSchema.ts` deliberately untouched this phase, per the same "standalone function now, superRefine once the gating conditions land" migration that file already documents at :658-661. **THE BRIEF'S MANDATED CROSS-CHECK AGAINST THE jq SSOT CAUGHT A REAL FALSE POSITIVE IN MY OWN PORT BEFORE IT SHIPPED:** the first version reported `FIX-COWORK-DISPATCH-ROUTER-INTENT-MUTEX-BYPASS` (supervised+plan_only, 38d, lane none) while `bounded1-supervised-lane-report.sh` correctly counted 0 — that row is an EPIC WRAPPER, which the report script's own READY-WRAPPER section documents as a "NO-PICKER-BY-DESIGN class" closed out by `post-cycle.md` § Step 4.4 Epic-Wrapper Autoclose Sweep, which is why its gating class is scoped `non-wrapper`. Added `isEpicWrapper` (inline OR detail `children[]`, with the same bare-string drift normalisation the jq `as_dep_array` does) and both implementations now agree at 0 on the live board. Tests: `node scripts/test-orch-validate-ac.mjs` 41/41 (29 baseline + 12 new AC-5) — positive cases for supervised-alone, plan_only-alone, empty-string-vs-absent handler shapes, and a detail-only flag; negative controls for the unflagged parked row (the row's own explicit non-goal), detail-only owner override, detail-only next_agent override, epic wrapper inline, epic wrapper via detail, and `in_progress[]` scope exclusion. New `ORCH_VALIDATE_DETAIL_PATH` env seam so the detail-override branch is provable without touching the real detail file. Live: `node scripts/orch-validate.mjs docs/data/orch/orch-state.json` exit 0, Stage 1i reports 0. No `apps/` code touched — `tsc` N/A this phase.

## [Developer] 2026-08-25 — FIX-CCATO-NTG-ROWS-NOT-PRODUCED-BY-EITHER-SANCTIONED-ENGINE-FORGED-WRITER-ID (cross-service/, developer, P0 M, incident-lane dispatch)
**AC-1 attribution — the row's headline premise is REFUTED.** Nothing forged the writer id. The ntg-* rows carrying the frozen ts `2026-08-24T00:00:00Z` were written by the REAL production writer (`writeNarrativeContradictionSignals` → `appendSignalQueueRow`), driven by `apps/mcp-server/src/__tests__/CCATO-MCP-T5-USECASE.test.ts`: five of its cases reach a FAIL verdict while overriding NEITHER `writeSignalsFn` NOR `orchStatePath`, so `runNarrativeTruthGate` fell back to the real writer at `DEFAULT_ORCH_STATE_PATH` (the live `docs/data/orch/orch-state.json`) with the suite's injected clock `new Date("2026-08-24T00:00:00Z")`. Every `bun test` run therefore appended exactly 6 rows to production state. Evidence: (a) the 6 rows first introduced at `ddab2f4c` carry `returned_value` multiset {60, 61, 61, 62.1, 62.1, "not found in database"} — the exact 1/2/2/1 ratio those 5 cases produce, matching PO's live 12/24/24/12 over 12 batches; (b) `claim`, `agent_id:"chef"` and the frozen ts are verbatim T5 fixture constants; (c) CAUSAL REPRO — removing only the new stub and re-running T5 fires exactly 6 `NTG-GUARD-REFUSED-TEST-HARNESS-LIVE-WRITE` lines. So "NEITHER sanctioned engine can have produced them" is false (the TS engine did), and the 7 "inverted verdicts" are T5's deliberate missing-`tool_null_markers` case, not a forgery.
**Fixes.** (1) `CCATO-MCP-T5-USECASE.test.ts` — `writeSignalsFn` stubbed in `baseDeps()` so no case in that file can leak. (2) New `apps/mcp-server/src/infrastructure/signals/narrativeContradictionGuards.ts` — write-boundary screening, quarantine + a distinct greppable marker per class, never a silent drop (AC-3): `NTG-GUARD-REFUSED-TEST-HARNESS-LIVE-WRITE` (NODE_ENV=test aimed at the live file), `NTG-GUARD-QUARANTINED-CLOCK-SKEW` (AC-7, |ts − wall clock| > 15 min, BOTH directions — the observed defect was 31h STALE, not future), `NTG-GUARD-QUARANTINED-NULL-MARKER-CONTRADICTION` (AC-4, `returned_value` contains a `claim-tool-map.json` `.tool_null_markers` entry, list read from the SSOT at runtime, never hardcoded). Sidecar: `docs/data/telemetry/narrative-contradiction-quarantine.jsonl` (gitignored). (3) AC-6 `dedup_key` = `narrative_contradiction:{agent_id}:{tool}:{ticker_or_dim}:{cycle}` emitted by BOTH engines, and honoured on append — `collapseOrPrepend()` in `orchStateStore.ts` and the jq reduce in `scripts/narrative-truth-gate.sh` bump `.occurrences`/`.last_seen_ts` instead of appending. Rows without a `dedup_key` are appended verbatim (zero change for the other 4 in-process callers). (4) AC-5 summary now QUOTES the probe evidence instead of asserting "returned non-null data" unconditionally; python and TS builders verified byte-identical on 4 shared cases.
**Verification.** New `FIX-CCATO-NTG-EMISSION-INTEGRITY.test.ts` 13/13. Replay of all 8 real ntg-* rows (6 leaked + the 2 live ones) at their OWN per-row emission wall clock: 6 BLOCKED (each independently caught by ≥2 guards), both genuine live rows ACCEPTED unchanged, 3 synthetic negative controls → 2 accepted + 1 collapsed to `occurrences=2`; zero genuine contradictions rejected. `bun tsc --noEmit` clean. 307/307 across all 19 test files touching the changed modules. ## [Developer] 2026-08-29 — FIX-ORCH-COLD-EVICT-VALIDATION-EXIT1 (scripts/, developer, P2 S, dev-team Step 3 direct-execute; telegram 5209, 2026-08-26): root-caused "cold eviction skipped, retry next tick" (exit 1 every tick) to `scripts/orch-apply.sh` running the conservation check BEFORE the CAS check — a cold-evict candidate built from a snapshot predating a peer's forward lane-moves (qa_drain etc.) scored as AC-3 "backward moves" against the fresher live file and aborted FATAL exit 1; the retryable CAS exit-2 check ran after and was never reached. Fix: CAS re-check moved BEFORE the Stage 2 conservation check (stale candidate → exit 2 → cold-evict retries against a fresh read → run completes; genuine violations still exit 1). Also fixed in `scripts/orch-cold-evict.sh`: (a) dry-run's `> /dev/stdout` pattern (fails hard in restricted/containerized environments) → real temp file; (b) live-path `mktemp` X-run suffix bug (BSD literal-name collision, same class as orch-apply's 5a18d8726). Reproduced the exact historical failing state (2026-08-26 20:38Z, 61 signal rows) — now evicts cleanly exit 0. Regression: TEST 14 in `scripts/test/orch-cold-evict-tests.sh` (stale candidate + peer forward moves → self-heals via CAS retry, peer moves preserved, terminal rows archived); T7 fixture repaired to DONE_VERIFIED (FIX-DONELANE made DONE rows in done[] unevictable by design, 2026-08-23). Wrapper suite 109/109, cold-evict suite 83/84 (1 environmental concurrent-writer flake on the real-file snapshot guard).
